Encoder 1311, world 3: decision 12

Gallery index

This gallery preserves all 552 scheduled first narrator positions and their 552 literal-template controls in the fixed export order across 12 archive cases. No favorable examples were selected. These are repeated reporting opportunities, not 552 independent worlds.

A_only is a hybrid record: copied W11 fields with the previous node replaced by a source current estimate. Conditional candidates inherit that record and replace the query node with a source forecast. Ordinary and analytic sources also model attention effects. Joined records additionally supply native W.

Narrator text is raw first narration, not certified faithful or conscious. Literal templates are deterministic controls. Incomplete, missing, invalid, or blank text remains explicitly unavailable or blank according to the unchanged pipeline._report_text helper; no partial response is promoted into a complete text. The exact returned text is HTML-escaped inside <pre> blocks. No prose-auditor or reference-rater outcomes are used.

01. s1311_w03_t12_A_A_only_baseline

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: baseline.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: fb1aa952f99d8641afef2e45ae4c2cde35941035b926da183e7fdfdd81e5d4b0.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 gives fallible 4x4 location distributions for blue and red at nodes n0–n3. Current records (represented_time 12) include copied prior records for n0–n2 (not new estimates) and a history estimate for n3. Current summaries: n0 — blue peak at cell (0,1) (peak probability ≈0.9823), red peak at (0,3) (≈0.999998). n1 and n2 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells. n3 (history) — blue peak at (1,2) (≈0.7672), red peak at (0,1) (≈0.5476). Candidate conditional command records k0, k1 and k2 provide alternative estimated distributions (they are conditional records, not executed commands). All candidate records preserve the same peak cells per role and node as the current records; however, peak probabilities and other distribution details differ across commands for n3 (e.g., blue and red peak probabilities vary between k0, k1 and k2).

02. s1311_w03_t12_A_A_only_current_donor

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: current_donor.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: d83bae0fec1895257b9ef90572742da007214e4cfba21392f7fcf6bd8cfc0bd7.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 current estimates (represented_time 12 or inherited): n0 — blue distribution peaks at cell [0,1] (peak probability ~0.9823), red peaks at [0,3] (~0.999998). n1 and n2 — both roles uniform across all 16 cells. n3 (history_estimate represented_time 12) — blue peaks at [1,2] (~0.9508), red peaks at [0,1] (~0.9922). Conditional candidate records (commands k0,k1,k2; represented_time 13 are command estimates and not events that have occurred; copied priors are not new estimates): for n0, n1 and n2 the candidate distributions match the current (same peaks). For n3 the candidates differ: k0 matches the current n3 peaks; k1 shifts the blue peak probability higher (~0.9762) and slightly lowers the red peak (~0.9872); k2 shifts the blue peak lower (~0.9454) and lowers the red peak further (~0.9793). No physical presences or events are inferred; numbers describe only the supplied distributions.

03. s1311_w03_t12_A_A_only_current_restored

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: current_restored.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: bca36e20bd49c43485983d7533186395c21542001781bd67c782068aa728644c.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 gives fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 (rows increase downward, columns increase rightward). Current records: n0 is a copied prior record (record_time 11, represented_time 12) with blue peaked at (0,1) and red peaked at (0,3); n1 and n2 are uniform over all 16 cells (copied prior record, represented_time 12); n3 is a history estimate (represented_time 12) with blue peaked at (1,2) and red peaked at (0,1). Candidate entries k0, k1 and k2 are conditional command estimates (feedback/derived as shown, not executed events). Their node-level peak cells largely match the current peaks: n0 peaks remain blue (0,1) and red (0,3); n1 and n2 remain uniform; n3 peaks remain blue (1,2) and red (0,1) across the k0–k2 conditionals, though the supplied conditional records report different entropies and peak probabilities. Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional records are not events that have already occurred.

11. s1311_w03_t12_A_joined_prospective_donor

Source: A; view: joined; variant: prospective_donor.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: 5fba1e723963c7da43f0fef8af17f92912973a0c4094f3cd229fdda3a283be04.

Narrator text:

This packet reports per-node, per-role discrete location distributions on separate 4x4 local grids. Origins: copied prior records are historical (not new estimates) and conditional command records are hypothetical branches (not executed events). Current (null command) summaries: n0 in both panels shows blue concentrated at cell (0,1) and red at (0,3); n1 and n2 are uniform across their grids for both roles; p0 current n3 concentrates blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1); p1 current n3 is uniform. Candidate (k0/k1/k2) records reproduce the same n0–n2 patterns; n3 varies by panel and command: p0 candidates consistently peak blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1) with peak probabilities near 0.95–0.99; p1 candidates include a uniform branch (k0) and branches with blue peaks on the lower-right quadrant or at (1,2) and corresponding red peaks on (0,1) or the upper-left quadrant. Differences across command-conditioned distributions are reflected above and in the per-distribution peak lists.

14. s1311_w03_t12_A_joined_W_restored

Source: A; view: joined; variant: W_restored.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: 2aaf07b6b8c31a9ce0bbe8642fa793235a9132c45d053801befe58b1d59af12b.

Narrator text:

Two panels (p0,p1) address nodes n0–n3 with probabilistic location distributions for red and blue on a 4×4 grid. Copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records are not executed events. Current summaries: p0 n0 — blue peaks at (0,1) 0.9823, red at (0,3) ≈0.999998; p0 n1 and n2 — both roles uniform across the grid; p0 n3 — blue peaks at (1,2) (≈0.77), red at (0,1) (≈0.55). p1 current: n0 like p0 n0, n1 and n2 uniform, n3 uniform. Conditional candidate records (k0,k1,k2) mostly reproduce the same peaks for n0–n2. Node n3 varies across commands: p0 n3 shows modest changes in peak probabilities between candidates; p1 n3 shows larger divergence across candidates (from uniform to moderately peaked to very strongly peaked at (1,2) for blue and (0,1) for red). These statements summarize the supplied distributions only.

In panel p1 n3 the candidate records differ in mode: k0 is uniform, k1 places blue mass on four cells and red mass on four different cells, and k2 concentrates blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1). Copied prior records are noted as inherited, not new observations.

29. s1311_w03_t12_analytic_A_only_baseline

Source: analytic; view: A_only; variant: baseline.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: 9ff79b1f65b682240d855c2eea473c8f5a9df735f98beae712dae111f184a847.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 — current: nodes n0–n3 contain 4×4 location distributions. The n0 distributions are copied from a prior record (record_time 11): blue peaks at cell [0,1] (high peak probability) and red peaks at [0,3] (very high peak probability). Nodes n1 and n2 in the current panel are uniform across all 16 cells. Node n3’s current entry is a history estimate (represented_time 12) and is uniform. Panel p0 — candidate command records k0, k1 and k2 are conditional command estimates (feedback_through 11, represented_time 13) and are not events that have occurred. Most candidate entries mirror the current/copied patterns; exceptions occur at node n3: under k1 blue peaks are the four top-right cells ([0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]) and red peaks are the four top-left cells ([0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]); under k2 blue peaks at [1,2] and red peaks at [0,1]. These statements describe only the supplied distributions and their origins, not physical facts or calibrated confidence.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.


Narrator text SHA-256: 74581e6a647ea99d2791bc74043b46e17f10bb5c5c21709fa9a8c3cdf0a6556a.


Narrator text:


This packet contains two panels (p0, p1). Each panel gives current 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 and three conditional candidate records (k0,k1,k2). Copied or completed prior records in the packet are not new estimates; candidate/conditional command records are hypothetical branches, not events that have occurred. Current (p0): n0 — blue peaks at cell (0,1), red at (0,3); n1 and n2 — uniform over all 16 cells; n3 — blue peaks at (1,2), red peaks at (0,1). Current (p1): n0 — blue (0,1) and red (0,3); n1,n2,n3 — uniform. Conditional differences: many candidate k0 entries match the current concentrated patterns at n0 (blue (0,1), red (0,3)) and uniform n1/n2; p0 k1 changes n3 to a 2×2 blue block at {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)} and a 2×2 red block at {(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)}; p0 k2 makes p0 n3 uniform. In p1 candidates, k1 gives a 2×2 blue block and 2×2 red block at n3, k2 gives blue peak (1,2) and red peak (0,1). No physical inferences are made; numbers describe distributions only.
